   the repair restores the tools;
3. describes the graph content: node and edge counts, edge types, how many node
   definitions are `async`, and, when results/audit/gold_symbols.jsonl exists,
   whether each function the reference patch changes is a node.

Run with the harness interpreter from scripts/setup_verify_env.sh (step 1 imports
swegemma). Files are fetched one task at a time and deleted after use. Output: one
JSON line per task in --out (resumable).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from g4bench import graphs, harness_env, symbols  # noqa: E402

COMP = "gemma-4-developer-agent"
ANCHOR = {"fastapi/fastapi": "FastAPI", "Textualize/rich": "Console", "psf/requests": "Session", "encode/httpx": "Client"}
# a node's OWN definition: optional decorator lines, then the def line, at the start of its text
_ASYNC_DEF = re.compile(r"\A(?:[ \t]*@[^\n]*\n)*[ \t]*async[ \t]+def[ \t]")
_SYNC_DEF = re.compile(r"\A(?:[ \t]*@[^\n]*\n)*[ \t]*def[ \t]")
_CONTAINS_ASYNC = re.compile(r"^[ \t]*async[ \t]+def[ \t]", re.M)


def fetch(name: str, size: int, dest_dir: Path) -> Path:
    """Place competition file `name` (e.g. graphs/x.json) in dest_dir. Empty files are created empty,
    as they are on the mounted dataset."""
    target = dest_dir / name
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if size == 0:
        target.write_bytes(b"")
        return target
    import time

    for attempt in range(4):
        subprocess.run(["kaggle", "competitions", "download", "-c", COMP, "-f", name, "-p", str(target.parent), "-q"],
                       capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=600)
        if target.exists() or (target.parent / (target.name + ".zip")).exists():
            break
        time.sleep(10 * (attempt + 1))
    z = target.parent / (target.name + ".zip")
    if z.exists():
        import zipfile
        with zipfile.ZipFile(z) as zf:
            zf.extractall(target.parent)
        z.unlink()
    if not target.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(f"download failed: {name}")
    return target


def issue_phrase(ps: str) -> str:
    for ln in ps.splitlines():
        ln = ln.strip().lstrip("#").strip()
        if len(ln) > 8:
            return ln[:80]
    return ps.strip()[:80]


def probe_tools(task: dict, gdir: Path, edir: Path) -> dict:
    harness_env.stub_model_serving()
    from swegemma.budget import EvaluationBudget, HarnessLimits
    from swegemma.context import SwegemmaContext
    from swegemma.graph import graph_utils, embedding_utils, retrieval_utils
    from swegemma.harness.agent_runner import build_agent_prompt
    from swegemma.models import Task
    from swegemma.tools.graph import get_code_neighbors, get_code_subgraph, search_similar_code

    # the harness caches graphs and embeddings in-process; clear them between layouts
    graph_utils.clear_caches()
    embedding_utils.clear_caches()
    retrieval_utils.clear_caches() if hasattr(retrieval_utils, "clear_caches") else None

    t = Task(instance_id=task["instance_id"], repo=task["repo"], base_commit=task["base_commit"], patch="",
             test_patch="", problem_statement=task["problem_statement"], hints_text="", created_at=task["created_at"])
    cfg = SimpleNamespace(graph_dir=str(gdir), embeddings_dir=str(edir), budget=EvaluationBudget(), harness=HarnessLimits(),
                          enable_sandbox_testing=True)
    prompt = build_agent_prompt(t, cfg, "")
    ctx = SwegemmaContext(task=t, repo=t.repo, graph_dir=str(gdir), embeddings_dir=str(edir),
                          budget=EvaluationBudget(time_minutes=60.0, tool_calls=10))
    anchor = ANCHOR[task["repo"]]
    calls = {
        "get_code_neighbors": lambda: get_code_neighbors(ctx, anchor),
        "get_code_subgraph": lambda: get_code_subgraph(ctx, [anchor]),
        "search_similar_code_symbol": lambda: search_similar_code(ctx, anchor),
        "search_similar_code_phrase": lambda: search_similar_code(ctx, issue_phrase(task["problem_statement"])),
    }
    out = {"prompt_has_graph_block": "## Code Intelligence Tools" in prompt}
    for k, fn in calls.items():
        try:
            r = json.loads(fn())
        except Exception as e:  # a crash is itself a result
            r = {"status": "crash", "error_type": type(e).__name__, "error_message": str(e)}
        out[k] = {
            "status": r.get("status"),
            "error_type": r.get("error_type"),
            "error_message": (r.get("error_message") or "")[:160] or None,
            "count": r.get("count"),
        }
    out["tool_calls_charged"] = ctx.tool_calls_used
    return out


def graph_content(path: Path, gold: dict | None) -> dict:
    data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    nodes = data.get("nodes", [])
    edges = data.get("edges", data.get("links", []))
    ids = {str(n.get("id")) for n in nodes}
    n_async = sum(1 for n in nodes if _ASYNC_DEF.match(n.get("text") or ""))
    n_sync = sum(1 for n in nodes if _SYNC_DEF.match(n.get("text") or ""))
    n_contains_async = sum(1 for n in nodes if _CONTAINS_ASYNC.search(n.get("text") or ""))
    out = {
        "nodes": len(nodes),
        "edges": len(edges),
        "edge_types": dict(Counter(str(e.get("type")) for e in edges)),
        "nodes_async_def": n_async,
        "nodes_sync_def": n_sync,
        "nodes_containing_async_def": n_contains_async,
    }
    if gold and "files" in gold:
        idx = symbols.build_suffix_index(ids)
        fn_syms = []
        for f in gold["files"]:
            for s in f.get("changed", []):
                if s["kind"] != "function":
                    continue
                sym = symbols.Symbol(s["module"], s["qualname"], s["kind"], s["async"])
                fn_syms.append({"symbol": sym.dotted, "async": s["async"], "in_graph": symbols.in_graph(sym, ids, idx)})
        out["gold_functions"] = fn_syms
        out["gold_functions_in_graph"] = sum(1 for s in fn_syms if s["in_graph"])
        out["gold_functions_total"] = len(fn_syms)
    return out


def recompute_content(out: Path, all_tasks: list[dict], sizes: dict, gold: dict) -> int:
    """Refetch each task's non-empty graph (either name, or a sibling task's) and redo `content`."""
    rows = [json.loads(ln) for ln in out.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if ln.strip()]
    by_id = {t["instance_id"]: t for t in all_tasks}
    for i, rec in enumerate(rows, 1):
        t = by_id[rec["instance_id"]]
        sibs = [u for u in all_tasks if u["repo"] == t["repo"] and u["base_commit"] == t["base_commit"]]
        cands = [graphs.task_file_names(t)["graph_commit"]] + [graphs.task_file_names(u)["graph_task"] for u in sibs]
        name = next((c for c in cands if sizes.get(c, 0) > 0), None)
        if name is None:
            rec["content"] = None
            continue
        tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="g4graph_"))
        try:
            rec["content"] = graph_content(fetch(name, sizes[name], tmp), gold.get(rec["instance_id"]))
            rec["content_source"] = name
        finally:
            shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)
        print(f"[{i}/{len(rows)}] {rec['instance_id']} async_nodes={rec['content']['nodes_async_def']} "
              f"gold={rec['content'].get('gold_functions_in_graph')}/{rec['content'].get('gold_functions_total')}", flush=True)
    out.write_text("".join(json.dumps(r, sort_keys=True) + "\n" for r in rows), encoding="utf-8")
    return 0


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--tasks", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--listing", required=True, help="JSON list of {name,size} for the competition files")
    ap.add_argument("--gold-symbols", default=str(HERE / "results" / "audit" / "gold_symbols.jsonl"))
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=str(HERE / "results" / "audit" / "graph_audit.jsonl"))
    ap.add_argument("--task-ids", default="")
    ap.add_argument("--no-content", action="store_true", help="skip graph-content statistics")
    ap.add_argument("--content-only", action="store_true",
                    help="recompute only the content statistics of existing records (tool probes untouched)")
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)

    tasks = [json.loads(ln) for ln in Path(args.tasks).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if ln.strip()]
    all_tasks = list(tasks)
    if args.task_ids:
        keep = set(args.task_ids.split(","))
        tasks = [t for t in tasks if t["instance_id"] in keep]
    sizes = graphs.load_listing(Path(args.listing))
    gold = {}
    gp = Path(args.gold_symbols)
    if gp.exists():
        for ln in gp.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
            if ln.strip():
                r = json.loads(ln)
                gold[r["instance_id"]] = r
    out = Path(args.out)
    if args.content_only:
        return recompute_content(out, all_tasks, sizes, gold)
    done = set()
    if out.exists():
        done = {json.loads(ln)["instance_id"] for ln in out.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if ln.strip()}
    for i, task in enumerate(tasks, 1):
        iid = task["instance_id"]
        if iid in done:
            continue
        names = graphs.task_file_names(task)
        # tasks sharing a base commit share the commit-named files; their content may sit
        # under a sibling task's name, so the repair step may need the sibling's files
        sibs = [u for u in all_tasks if u["repo"] == task["repo"] and u["base_commit"] == task["base_commit"] and u["instance_id"] != iid]
        tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="g4graph_"))
        try:
            local = {k: fetch(v, sizes.get(v, -1) if sizes.get(v, -1) >= 0 else 0, tmp) for k, v in names.items()}
            sib_local = {}
            for u in sibs:
                for k, v in graphs.task_file_names(u).items():
                    if k.endswith("_task"):
                        sib_local.setdefault(k, []).append(fetch(v, sizes.get(v, 0), tmp))
            rec = {"instance_id": iid, "repo": task["repo"],
                   "sizes": {k: sizes.get(v) for k, v in names.items()},
                   "as_shipped": probe_tools(task, tmp / "graphs", tmp / "embeddings")}
            # repair: content to both names
            for kind in ("graph", "embedding"):
                a, b = local[f"{kind}_commit"], local[f"{kind}_task"]
                pool = [a, b] + sib_local.get(f"{kind}_task", [])
                src = next((x for x in pool if x.stat().st_size > 0), a)
                for dst in (a, b):
                    if dst.stat().st_size == 0 and src.stat().st_size > 0:
                        shutil.copyfile(src, dst)
            rec["repaired"] = probe_tools(task, tmp / "graphs", tmp / "embeddings")
            rec["repair_used_sibling"] = bool(sibs)
            if not args.no_content:
                g = local["graph_commit"]
                if g.stat().st_size > 0:
                    rec["content"] = graph_content(g, gold.get(iid))
                else:
                    rec["content"] = None
        except Exception as e:
            rec = {"instance_id": iid, "repo": task["repo"], "error": f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"[:300]}
        finally:
            shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)
        if "download failed" in rec.get("error", ""):
            print(f"skipping {iid}: {rec['error']}", flush=True)  # not recorded, so a rerun retries it
            continue
        with out.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            fh.write(json.dumps(rec, sort_keys=True) + "\n")
        a = rec.get("as_shipped", {})
        print(f"[{i}/{len(tasks)}] {iid} block={a.get('prompt_has_graph_block')} "
              f"neighbors={a.get('get_code_neighbors', {}).get('status')} "
              f"search={a.get('search_similar_code_symbol', {}).get('status')} "
              f"repaired_block={rec.get('repaired', {}).get('prompt_has_graph_block')}", flush=True)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

# CELL 16 code
%%writefile g4src/scripts/gold_symbols_from_snapshot.py
"""`gold_symbols`, copied unchanged from scripts/verify_scorer.py at commit 7a286d8a72a6.

The rest of verify_scorer.py runs the official verifier and needs the harness; it is not
used here. The notebook checks the function text against a SHA-256 of the original."""

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
import tarfile
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))

from g4bench import diffs, symbols  # noqa: E402


def gold_symbols(task: dict, tgz: Path) -> dict:
    """Symbols enclosing the reference patch's changed lines, read from the snapshot."""
    fds = diffs.parse_diff(task["patch"])
    code = [f for f in fds if f.path.endswith(".py") and not diffs.is_test_path(f.path)]
    existing = {f.path: f for f in code if not f.is_new}
    sources: dict[str, str] = {}
    if existing:
        with tarfile.open(tgz, "r:gz") as tf:
            for m in tf:
                name = m.name[2:] if m.name.startswith("./") else m.name
                if name in existing and m.isfile():
                    fh = tf.extractfile(m)
                    if fh is not None:
                        sources[name] = fh.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")
                    if len(sources) == len(existing):
                        break
    files = []
    for f in code:
        rec: dict = {"path": f.path, "new_file": f.is_new, "added": f.added, "removed": f.removed}
        if f.is_new:
            rec["changed"] = []
        else:
            src = sources.get(f.path)
            lines = sorted({ln for h in f.hunks for ln in h.changed_old_lines()})
            if src is None:
                rec["error"] = "file not in snapshot"
                rec["changed"] = []
            else:
                syms = symbols.enclosing_symbols(f.path, src, lines)
                if not syms and lines:
                    rec["error"] = "source did not parse"
                rec["changed"] = [
                    {"module": s.module, "qualname": s.qualname, "kind": s.kind, "async": s.is_async} for s in syms
                ]
        old_defs = {n for n, _ in diffs.defined_symbols(sources.get(f.path, "").splitlines())}
        rec["added_defs"] = [
            {"name": n, "async": a} for n, a in diffs.defined_symbols([x for h in f.hunks for x in h.added]) if n not in old_defs
        ]
        files.append(rec)
    return {"instance_id": task["instance_id"], "repo": task["repo"], "files": files}

# CELL 17 code
SOURCE_COMMIT = "7a286d8a72a6e30f8d2f1d5b1b924dde40d95f77"

GOLD_SYMBOLS_FUNCTION_SHA256 = "e29d7a3adcc1c70b5b63a62e88ac28a51ece179aab7a87bb79f832e1eb549591"  # the function text in scripts/verify_scorer.py


def sha256_bytes(b: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()


bad = [rel for rel, h in SOURCE_SHA256.items() if sha256_bytes((WORK / "g4src" / rel).read_bytes()) != h]
if bad:
    raise RuntimeError(f"vendored files differ from commit {SOURCE_COMMIT[:12]}: {bad}")
_src = (WORK / "g4src/scripts/gold_symbols_from_snapshot.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
_fn = next(n for n in ast.parse(_src).body if isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name == "gold_symbols")
if sha256_bytes(ast.get_source_segment(_src, _fn).encode("utf-8")) != GOLD_SYMBOLS_FUNCTION_SHA256:
    raise RuntimeError("gold_symbols differs from scripts/verify_scorer.py at SOURCE_COMMIT")


def load(name: str, rel: str):
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, WORK / "g4src" / rel)
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[name] = mod
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod


AT = load("audit_tasks", "scripts/audit_tasks.py")
PT = load("power_table", "scripts/power_table.py")
AG = load("audit_graphs", "scripts/audit_graphs.py")
GS = load("gold_symbols_from_snapshot", "scripts/gold_symbols_from_snapshot.py")
from g4bench import graphs as G  # noqa: E402  (the scripts put g4src/ on sys.path)

print(f"{len(SOURCE_SHA256)} vendored files match commit {SOURCE_COMMIT[:12]}; gold_symbols matches verify_scorer.py")

    "power/power_table.md": "d72bc703e9c304a26267d800592fe5e1ccc52738a4a977c430645cd28979b5d8",

def recorded(name: str) -> str:
    origin, h, b64 = RECORDED[name]
    text = gzip.decompress(base64.b64decode(b64)).decode("utf-8")
    if sha256_bytes(text.encode("utf-8")) != h:
        raise RuntimeError(f"recorded {name}: checksum mismatch")
    return text


def read_jsonl_text(text: str) -> list:
    return [json.loads(ln) for ln in text.splitlines() if ln.strip()]


for name, (origin, h, _) in RECORDED.items():
    recorded(name)
    print(f"  {name:34s} {h[:12]}  from {origin[:90]}")
# audit_tasks.py reads the verifier outcomes from <its root>/results/verification/runs.jsonl
_ = (WORK / "g4src/results/verification/runs.jsonl").write_text(recorded("verification/runs.jsonl"), encoding="utf-8")

# CELL 20 markdown
## 4. The file listing: which graph and embedding files were empty, as first published

The harness decides what an agent gets from the sizes of the graph and embedding files. The
Writeup's figures are for the files as first published, so the tables in sections 6 to 9 are
built from the listing recorded through the Kaggle API on 24 September 2026 (782 files), whatever
is attached. The attached files are measured and compared with that listing and with the listing
recorded after the re-issue (26 September 2026, 524 files); section 10 uses the measurement.

# CELL 21 code
rec_listing = json.loads(recorded("audit/file_listing.json"))
rec_sizes = {r["name"]: int(r["size"]) for r in rec_listing}
re_listing = json.loads(recorded("reissue/file_listing.json"))
re_sizes_rec = {r["name"]: int(r["size"]) for r in re_listing}
measured = {}
for sub in ("graphs", "embeddings", "snapshots"):
    d = DATA / sub
    if d.is_dir():
        for e in os.scandir(d):
            if e.is_file():
                measured[f"{sub}/{e.name}"] = e.stat().st_size

GE = ("graphs/", "embeddings/")


def graph_files(d: dict) -> dict:
    return {k: v for k, v in d.items() if k.startswith(GE)}


GRAPHS_ATTACHED = (DATA / "graphs").is_dir() and (DATA / "embeddings").is_dir()
if not GRAPHS_ATTACHED:
    ATTACHED = "not attached"
elif graph_files(measured) == graph_files(rec_sizes):
    ATTACHED = "the first release"
elif graph_files(measured) == graph_files(re_sizes_rec):
    ATTACHED = "the re-issue"
else:
    ATTACHED = "NEITHER recorded release"
sizes = dict(rec_sizes)  # the tables: the files as first published, always

for sub in ("graphs", "embeddings", "snapshots"):
    mea = {k: v for k, v in measured.items() if k.startswith(sub + "/")}
    print(f"{sub}/ attached: " + (f"{len(mea)} files, {sum(1 for v in mea.values() if v == 0)} of 0 bytes, "
                                  f"{sum(mea.values()) / 1e6:,.1f} MB" if mea else "not attached"))
    for label, ref in (("first-release listing (24 Sep)", rec_sizes), ("re-issue listing (26 Sep)", re_sizes_rec)):
        rec = {k: v for k, v in ref.items() if k.startswith(sub + "/")}
        line = (f"    {label}: {len(rec)} files, {sum(1 for v in rec.values() if v == 0)} of 0 bytes, "
                f"{sum(rec.values()) / 1e6:,.1f} MB")
        if mea:
            diff = sum(1 for k in rec.keys() & mea.keys() if rec[k] != mea[k])
            line += (f"; attached files against it: {len(rec.keys() - mea.keys())} missing, "
                     f"{len(mea.keys() - rec.keys())} extra, {diff} of a different size")
        print(line)
print(f"Graph and embedding files attached: {ATTACHED}")

LISTING_PATH = WORK / "g4_audit/audit/file_listing_used.json"
_ = LISTING_PATH.write_text(json.dumps(sorted(({"name": k, "size": v} for k, v in sizes.items()), key=lambda r: r["name"]),
                                       indent=0) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
same = {r["name"]: r["size"] for r in json.loads(LISTING_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))} == rec_sizes
print(f"Listing used for the tables, as first published: {len(sizes)} files, "
      f"{'the same names and sizes as' if same else 'DIFFERENT from'} the recorded first-release listing")

# CELL 22 markdown
## 5. The functions each reference patch changes

For every task, the changed lines of the reference patch are mapped to the innermost enclosing
definition in the base-commit source, read from the task's snapshot archive. Only the names of
those definitions are kept. Without the archives, the recorded result is used.

# CELL 23 code
tasks = read_jsonl_text((DATA / MARKER).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
print(f"{len(tasks)} tasks: " + ", ".join(f"{k} {v}" for k, v in Counter(t["repo"] for t in tasks).most_common()))

rec_gold_rows = read_jsonl_text(recorded("audit/gold_symbols.jsonl"))
rec_gold = {r["instance_id"]: r for r in rec_gold_rows}
gold, gold_origin, gold_errors, gold_differ = {}, Counter(), Counter(), []
t1 = time.time()
for i, t in enumerate(tasks, 1):
    iid = t["instance_id"]
    tgz = DATA / "snapshots" / f"{iid}.tgz"
    if RECOMPUTE_GOLD_SYMBOLS and tgz.is_file():
        try:
            r = GS.gold_symbols(t, tgz)
        except Exception as e:  # an unreadable archive is counted and the recorded value used
            gold_errors[type(e).__name__] += 1
        else:
            gold[iid] = r
            gold_origin["recomputed"] += 1
            if r != rec_gold.get(iid):
                gold_differ.append(iid)
    if iid not in gold:
        gold[iid] = rec_gold[iid]
        gold_origin["recorded"] += 1
    if gold_origin["recomputed"] and i % 20 == 0:
        print(f"  {i}/{len(tasks)} tasks, {time.time() - t1:.0f} s")

print(f"Changed functions: recomputed from snapshots for {gold_origin['recomputed']} tasks, "
      f"recorded values used for {gold_origin['recorded']}; unreadable archives: {dict(gold_errors) or 0}")
print(f"Recomputed records that differ from the recorded ones: {len(gold_differ)} {gold_differ[:10]}")
order = [r["instance_id"] for r in rec_gold_rows] + [t["instance_id"] for t in tasks if t["instance_id"] not in rec_gold]
_ = (WORK / "g4_audit/audit/gold_symbols.jsonl").write_text(
    "".join(json.dumps(gold[i], sort_keys=True) + "\n" for i in order), encoding="utf-8")

# CELL 24 markdown
## 6. The graph audit, as first published

Which graph and embedding files the harness reads follows from the first-release listing
(section 4). What the graphs contain is recomputed here from the attached graph files: for each
task, the graph that holds its content, its node and edge counts, how many nodes are `async`
definitions, and whether each changed function is a node. What the tools return when called
through the harness is the recorded probe.

The content is compared with the record on content only. As first published, 69 tasks found
their content only under their own or a sibling task's name; the re-issue removed those names,
so on the re-issued files the same content is read from the commit-named file. The table keeps,
in `content_source`, the file that held the content as first published, and the cell prints how
many tasks were read from another file.

# CELL 25 code
rec_ga_rows = read_jsonl_text(recorded("audit/graph_audit.jsonl"))
graph_origin, graph_errors, graph_differ, graph_read_from = Counter(), Counter(), [], Counter()
RECOMPUTED_CONTENT = {}  # instance_id -> content recomputed from an attached graph file
by_commit = defaultdict(list)
for t in tasks:
    by_commit[(t["repo"], t["base_commit"])].append(t)
task_by_id = {t["instance_id"]: t for t in tasks}
READ_SIZES = measured if GRAPHS_ATTACHED else sizes  # which attached file holds a task's content
t1 = time.time()
ga_rows = []
for rec in rec_ga_rows:
    t = task_by_id[rec["instance_id"]]
    sibs = by_commit[(t["repo"], t["base_commit"])]
    cands = [G.task_file_names(t)["graph_commit"]] + [G.task_file_names(u)["graph_task"] for u in sibs]
    name = next((c for c in cands if (READ_SIZES.get(c) or 0) > 0), None)
    row, done = dict(rec), False
    path = DATA / name if name else None
    if RECOMPUTE_GRAPH_CONTENT and path is not None and path.is_file():
        try:
            content = AG.graph_content(path, gold.get(t["instance_id"]))
        except Exception as e:  # an unreadable graph is counted and the recorded value used
            graph_errors[type(e).__name__] += 1
        else:
            row["content"], done = content, True
            RECOMPUTED_CONTENT[t["instance_id"]] = content
            graph_read_from["the file named in the record" if name == rec.get("content_source")
                            else "another file (the commit-named copy, after the re-issue)"] += 1
            if content != rec.get("content"):
                graph_differ.append(t["instance_id"])
    graph_origin["recomputed" if done else "recorded"] += 1
    ga_rows.append(row)
print(f"Graph content: recomputed for {graph_origin['recomputed']} tasks, recorded values used for "
      f"{graph_origin['recorded']}; unreadable graphs: {dict(graph_errors) or 0}; {time.time() - t1:.0f} s")
print(f"Recomputed content read from: {dict(graph_read_from) or 'no file'}")
print(f"Recomputed content that differs from the recorded: {len(graph_differ)} {graph_differ[:10]}")
probe_stale = [r["instance_id"] for r in rec_ga_rows
               if {k: sizes.get(v) for k, v in G.task_file_names(task_by_id[r["instance_id"]]).items()} != r.get("sizes")]
print(f"Tasks whose first-release file sizes differ from those the recorded tool probe saw: "
      f"{len(probe_stale)} {probe_stale[:10]}")
GA_PATH = WORK / "g4_audit/audit/graph_audit.jsonl"
GA_PATH.write_text("".join(json.dumps(r, sort_keys=True) + "\n" for r in ga_rows), encoding="utf-8")

# the graph-audit table, by repository
REPOS = ["fastapi/fastapi", "Textualize/rich", "psf/requests", "encode/httpx"]
cls = {t["instance_id"]: G.classify(t, sizes)["class"] for t in tasks}
agg = {k: Counter() for k in REPOS + ["all"]}
edge_types = Counter()
for r in ga_rows:
    c, a, rp = r["content"] or {}, r["as_shipped"], r["repaired"]
    for key in (r["repo"], "all"):
        g = agg[key]
        g["tasks"] += 1
        g["class_" + cls[r["instance_id"]][0]] += 1
        g["function_nodes"] += c.get("nodes_sync_def", 0) + c.get("nodes_async_def", 0)
        g["async_def_nodes"] += c.get("nodes_async_def", 0)
        g["nodes_enclosing_async_def"] += c.get("nodes_containing_async_def", 0)
        fns = c.get("gold_functions", [])
        g["changed_sync"] += sum(1 for f in fns if not f["async"])
        g["changed_sync_in_graph"] += sum(1 for f in fns if not f["async"] and f["in_graph"])
        g["changed_async"] += sum(1 for f in fns if f["async"])
        g["changed_async_in_graph"] += sum(1 for f in fns if f["async"] and f["in_graph"])
        g["tasks_changing_async"] += any(f["async"] for f in fns)
        g["shipped_block"] += bool(a["prompt_has_graph_block"])
        g["shipped_neighbors_ok"] += a["get_code_neighbors"]["status"] == "ok"
        g["shipped_symbol_search_results"] += (a["search_similar_code_symbol"]["count"] or 0) > 0
        g["shipped_phrase_search_results"] += (a["search_similar_code_phrase"]["count"] or 0) > 0
        g["repaired_block"] += bool(rp["prompt_has_graph_block"])
        g["repaired_neighbors_ok"] += rp["get_code_neighbors"]["status"] == "ok"
        g["repaired_symbol_search_results"] += (rp["search_similar_code_symbol"]["count"] or 0) > 0
        g["repaired_phrase_search_results"] += (rp["search_similar_code_phrase"]["count"] or 0) > 0
    edge_types.update(c.get("edge_types", {}))
GRAPH_SUMMARY = {k: dict(v) for k, v in agg.items()}
GRAPH_SUMMARY["all"]["edge_types"] = dict(edge_types)
GRAPH_SUMMARY["all"]["distinct_graphs_read"] = len({r["content_source"] for r in ga_rows if r.get("content")})

LABELS = [
    ("tasks", "Tasks"), ("class_A", "A: graph and embeddings readable"), ("class_B", "B: embeddings empty"),
    ("class_C", "C: graph empty"), ("class_D", "D: both empty"),
    ("shipped_block", "As first shipped: code-intelligence block in the prompt"),
    ("shipped_neighbors_ok", "As first shipped: get_code_neighbors succeeds"),
    ("shipped_symbol_search_results", "As first shipped: search_similar_code(symbol) returns results"),
    ("shipped_phrase_search_results", "As first shipped: search_similar_code(issue phrase) returns results"),
    ("repaired_block", "Repaired: block in the prompt"), ("repaired_neighbors_ok", "Repaired: get_code_neighbors succeeds"),
    ("repaired_symbol_search_results", "Repaired: symbol search returns results"),
    ("repaired_phrase_search_results", "Repaired: phrase search returns results"),
    ("function_nodes", "Function nodes (def and async def)"), ("async_def_nodes", "Nodes that are an async def"),
    ("nodes_enclosing_async_def", "Nodes whose text contains an async def"),
    ("changed_sync", "Changed synchronous functions"), ("changed_sync_in_graph", "  of which graph nodes"),
    ("changed_async", "Changed async functions"), ("changed_async_in_graph", "  of which graph nodes"),
    ("tasks_changing_async", "Tasks that change an async function"),
]
L = ["| Measure | " + " | ".join(r.split("/")[1] for r in REPOS) + " | All |", "|---|" + "---|" * (len(REPOS) + 1)]
for k, label in LABELS:
    L.append(f"| {label} | " + " | ".join(f"{GRAPH_SUMMARY[r].get(k, 0):,}" for r in REPOS + ["all"]) + " |")
L.append(f"\nEdge types over all graphs: {dict(edge_types)}. Distinct graph files read: {GRAPH_SUMMARY['all']['distinct_graphs_read']}.")
GRAPH_MD = "# Graph audit, as first published\n\n" + "\n".join(L) + "\n"
_ = (WORK / "g4_audit/audit/graph_audit_summary.md").write_text(GRAPH_MD, encoding="utf-8")
_ = (WORK / "g4_audit/audit/graph_audit_summary.json").write_text(
    json.dumps(GRAPH_SUMMARY, indent=1, sort_keys=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
display(Markdown(GRAPH_MD))

# CELL 30 markdown
## 9. Comparison with the committed tables

Each regenerated file against its SHA-256 at the source commit. Where a file differs, the
differing fields are listed.

# CELL 31 code
import csv
import io


def flat(x, prefix=""):
    if isinstance(x, dict):
        out = {}
        for k, v in x.items():
            out.update(flat(v, f"{prefix}.{k}" if prefix else str(k)))
        return out
    if isinstance(x, list) and x and isinstance(x[0], dict):
        out = {}
        for i, v in enumerate(x):
            out.update(flat(v, f"{prefix}[{i}]"))
        return out
    return {prefix: x}


def json_diff(new_text: str, old_text: str, limit: int = 30) -> list:
    a, b = flat(json.loads(new_text)), flat(json.loads(old_text))
    keys = sorted(k for k in a.keys() | b.keys() if a.get(k, "<absent>") != b.get(k, "<absent>"))
    return [f"    {k}: regenerated {a.get(k, '<absent>')!r}, committed {b.get(k, '<absent>')!r}" for k in keys[:limit]]


COMPARISON = {}
for rel in COMMITTED_SHA256:
    p = WORK / "g4_audit" / rel
    same = sha256_bytes(p.read_bytes()) == COMMITTED_SHA256[rel]
    COMPARISON[rel] = same
    print(f"{rel:28s} {'identical to the committed file' if same else 'DIFFERS from the committed file'}")
    if same:
        continue
    new = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if rel == "audit/summary.json":
        print("\n".join(json_diff(new, recorded("committed/audit/summary.json"))))
    elif rel == "power/power_table.json":
        print("\n".join(json_diff(new, recorded("committed/power/power_table.json"))))
    elif rel == "audit/tasks_audit.csv":
        a = {r["instance_id"]: r for r in csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(new))}
        b = {r["instance_id"]: r for r in csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(recorded("committed/audit/tasks_audit.csv")))}
        cols = Counter()
        for i in a.keys() & b.keys():
            cols.update(k for k in a[i] if a[i][k] != b[i].get(k))
        print(f"    rows only regenerated {len(a.keys() - b.keys())}, only committed {len(b.keys() - a.keys())}; "
              f"differing cells by column: {dict(cols)}")
print(f"\n{sum(COMPARISON.values())} of {len(COMPARISON)} regenerated files identical to the committed ones")

# CELL 32 markdown
## 10. The 25 September re-issue

On 25 September 2026 Kaggle re-uploaded the competition data and the harness wheels. This
section measures the re-issued graph and embedding files and compares them with the first
release:

* which files exist, whether any is empty, and the class the harness's rule gives each task.
  The sizes are measured on the attached files when those are not the first release's;
  otherwise the listing recorded on 26 September is used, and the cell says which;
* the graph content: recomputed in section 6 from the attached files when those are the
  re-issue, otherwise taken from the recorded re-issue probe, and compared with the first
  release's content;
* the harness tool probe run on the re-issued files with the re-issued wheels (recorded,
  `scripts/audit_graphs.py` unchanged), and whether the sizes it saw match the sizes here;
* the official verifier re-run on the re-issued data for 8 tasks, both arms, in the published
  and completed2_pinned environments (32 runs, recorded), against the first release's decisions.

# CELL 33 code
re_probe = read_jsonl_text(recorded("reissue/graph_audit.jsonl"))
re_runs = read_jsonl_text(recorded("reissue/runs.jsonl"))
first_runs = read_jsonl_text(recorded("verification/runs.jsonl"))

if GRAPHS_ATTACHED and ATTACHED != "the first release":
    RE_SIZES_ORIGIN = "measured on the attached files"
    re_sizes = {k: v for k, v in re_sizes_rec.items() if not k.startswith(GE)}
    re_sizes.update(graph_files(measured))
else:
    RE_SIZES_ORIGIN = ("the re-issue listing recorded on 26 September ("
                       + ("the attached graph files are the first release's" if GRAPHS_ATTACHED
                          else "no graph files are attached") + ")")
    re_sizes = dict(re_sizes_rec)
print(f"Graph and embedding sizes, as re-issued: {RE_SIZES_ORIGIN}")

names = {t["instance_id"]: G.task_file_names(t) for t in tasks}
re_g = [k for k in re_sizes if k.startswith("graphs/")]
re_e = [k for k in re_sizes if k.startswith("embeddings/")]
REISSUE = {
    "sizes_from": RE_SIZES_ORIGIN,
    "commits": len({(t["repo"], t["base_commit"]) for t in tasks}),
    "graph_files": len(re_g),
    "graph_files_empty": sum(1 for k in re_g if re_sizes[k] == 0),
    "embedding_files": len(re_e),
    "embedding_files_empty": sum(1 for k in re_e if re_sizes[k] == 0),
    "task_named_files_left": sum(1 for n in names.values() for k in ("graph_task", "embedding_task") if n[k] in re_sizes),
    "tasks_whose_commit_has_a_nonempty_graph_and_embedding": sum(
        1 for n in names.values() if (re_sizes.get(n["graph_commit"]) or 0) > 0 and (re_sizes.get(n["embedding_commit"]) or 0) > 0),
}
REISSUE["classes"] = dict(sorted(Counter(G.classify(t, re_sizes)["class"] for t in tasks).items()))

# the graph content: recomputed from the attached re-issued files, or the recorded re-issue probe's
first_content = {r["instance_id"]: r["content"] for r in rec_ga_rows}
re_content, re_content_origin = {}, Counter()
for r in re_probe:
    iid = r["instance_id"]
    if GRAPHS_ATTACHED and ATTACHED != "the first release" and iid in RECOMPUTED_CONTENT:
        re_content[iid] = RECOMPUTED_CONTENT[iid]
        re_content_origin["recomputed from the attached files"] += 1
    else:
        re_content[iid] = r["content"]
        re_content_origin["recorded re-issue probe"] += 1
REISSUE["content_from"] = dict(re_content_origin)
REISSUE["content_identical_to_first_release"] = sum(1 for i, c in re_content.items() if c == first_content.get(i))
REISSUE["recorded_content_identical_to_first_release"] = sum(1 for r in re_probe if r["content"] == first_content.get(r["instance_id"]))
REISSUE["function_nodes"] = sum((c or {}).get("nodes_sync_def", 0) + (c or {}).get("nodes_async_def", 0) for c in re_content.values())
REISSUE["async_def_nodes"] = sum((c or {}).get("nodes_async_def", 0) for c in re_content.values())

# the recorded probe on the re-issued files with the re-issued wheels
sh = [r["as_shipped"] for r in re_probe]
first_repaired = {r["instance_id"]: r["repaired"] for r in rec_ga_rows}
REISSUE["probe_tasks"] = len(re_probe)
REISSUE["probe_block"] = sum(1 for a in sh if a["prompt_has_graph_block"])
REISSUE["probe_neighbors_and_subgraph_ok"] = sum(1 for a in sh if a["get_code_neighbors"]["status"] == "ok"
                                                  and a["get_code_subgraph"]["status"] == "ok")
REISSUE["probe_symbol_search_results"] = sum(1 for a in sh if (a["search_similar_code_symbol"]["count"] or 0) > 0)
REISSUE["probe_phrase_search_results"] = sum(1 for a in sh if (a["search_similar_code_phrase"]["count"] or 0) > 0)
REISSUE["probe_tasks_with_a_tool_error"] = sum(1 for a in sh if any(
    a[k]["status"] != "ok" for k in ("get_code_neighbors", "get_code_subgraph", "search_similar_code_symbol", "search_similar_code_phrase")))
REISSUE["probe_equal_to_first_release_after_repair"] = sum(1 for r in re_probe if r["as_shipped"] == first_repaired.get(r["instance_id"]))
REISSUE["probe_sizes_differ_from_here"] = sorted(r["instance_id"] for r in re_probe
                                                 if {k: re_sizes.get(v) for k, v in names[r["instance_id"]].items()} != r["sizes"])

# the verifier re-run on the re-issued data, against the first release's decisions
first_dec = {(r["instance_id"], r["env"], r["arm"]): r["resolved"] for r in first_runs}
pairs = [(r, first_dec.get((r["instance_id"], r["env"].replace("-r0925", ""), r["arm"]))) for r in re_runs]
REISSUE["rerun_runs"] = len(re_runs)
REISSUE["rerun_tasks"] = len({r["instance_id"] for r in re_runs})
REISSUE["rerun_envs"] = sorted({r["env"] for r in re_runs})
REISSUE["rerun_decisions_identical"] = sum(1 for r, d in pairs if d is not None and r["resolved"] == d)
REISSUE["rerun_reimplementation_agrees"] = sum(1 for r in re_runs if r["agree"])

(WORK / "g4_audit/reissue").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
_ = (WORK / "g4_audit/reissue/reissue_summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(REISSUE, indent=1) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
display(Markdown("\n".join(["| Re-issue measure | Value |", "|---|---|"]
                           + [f"| {k.replace('_', ' ')} | {v} |" for k, v in REISSUE.items()])))

# CELL 35 code
S = json.loads((WORK / "g4_audit/audit/summary.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
P = json.loads((WORK / "g4_audit/power/power_table.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
ROWS = json.loads((WORK / "g4_audit/audit/tasks_audit.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["rows"]
RUNS = read_jsonl_text((WORK / "g4src/results/verification/runs.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
GA = GRAPH_SUMMARY
CHECKS = []


def check(section, what, quoted, value, ok=None):
    ok = str(quoted) == str(value) if ok is None else bool(ok)
    CHECKS.append((section, what, str(quoted), str(value), ok))


def v3(tag):
    v = S[f"verification_{tag}"]
    return f"{v['gold_resolved']} / {v['empty_resolved']} / {v['valid_pairs']}"


def by_repo(tag, repo):
    c = S[f"verification_{tag}"]["by_repo"][repo]
    return f"{c['gold_resolved']} / {c['empty_resolved']}"


def missing(module, repo=None):
    return sum(1 for r in ROWS if (repo is None or r["repo"] == repo)
               and module in (r["verify_published_missing"] or "").split(";"))


def imported(repo):
    c = Counter(r["verify_published_imported_from"] for r in ROWS if r["repo"] == repo)
    k, n = c.most_common(1)[0]
    return f"{k} {n} of {sum(c.values())}"


def arms(env):
    # {instance_id: resolved} for the reference fix and the empty patch, from the recorded runs
    return tuple({r["instance_id"]: r["resolved"] for r in RUNS if r["env"] == env and r["arm"] == a} for a in ("gold", "empty"))


def v3_runs(env):
    g, e = arms(env)
    return f"{sum(g.values())} / {sum(e.values())} / {sum(1 for i in g if g[i] and e.get(i) is False)}"


def version(name):
    return tuple(int(x) for x in name.split("-")[1].split(".") if x.isdigit())


pub = [r for r in RUNS if r["env"] == "published"]
wheels = {k.split("/", 1)[1]: v for k, v in sizes.items() if k.startswith("wheels/")}
check("2", "dependency wheels: count (MB)", "124 (28)", f"{len(wheels)} ({sum(wheels.values()) / 1e6:.0f})")
check("4.1", "tasks by repository", "fastapi 67, rich 48, requests 13, httpx 1",
      ", ".join(f"{k.split('/')[1]} {v}" for k, v in sorted(S["by_repo"].items(), key=lambda kv: -kv[1])))
check("4.1", "published: gold / empty / valid", "43 / 3 / 40", v3("published"))
for repo, q in (("psf/requests", "3 / 3"), ("encode/httpx", "0 / 0"), ("Textualize/rich", "39 / 0"), ("fastapi/fastapi", "1 / 0")):
    check("4.1", f"published, {repo}: gold / empty", q, by_repo("published", repo))
check("4.1", "requests imported from", "site-packages 13 of 13", imported("psf/requests"))
check("4.1", "rich imported from", "workspace 48 of 48", imported("Textualize/rich"))
_rh = [r for r in ROWS if r["repo"] in ("psf/requests", "encode/httpx")]
check("4.1", "requests and httpx: the release imported (site-packages)", "14 of 14",
      f"{sum(1 for r in _rh if r['verify_published_imported_from'] == 'site-packages')} of {len(_rh)}")
check("4.1", "empty patch resolves (published)", "requests_7309, requests_7315, requests_7427",
      ", ".join(sorted(r["instance_id"] for r in ROWS if r["verify_published_empty"])))
check("4.1", "highest released requests / httpx / pydantic wheels", "2.34.2 / 0.28.1 / 2.13.4",
      " / ".join(".".join(map(str, max(version(w) for w in wheels if w.lower().startswith(p + "-"))))
                 for p in ("requests", "httpx", "pydantic")))
check("4.1", "fastapi tasks missing typing_inspection", 63, missing("typing_inspection", "fastapi/fastapi"))
check("4.1", "tasks missing dirty_equals / attrs", "9 / 1", f"{missing('dirty_equals')} / {missing('attr')}")
check("4.1", "requests tasks missing the httpbin fixture", 8, missing("fixture:httpbin", "psf/requests"))
_st = [version(w) for w in wheels if w.lower().startswith("starlette-")]
check("4.1", "starlette releases among the wheels / highest", "56 / 1.6.0", f"{len(_st)} / {'.'.join(map(str, max(_st)))}")
check("4.1", "completed: gold / empty / valid", "55 / 4 / 51", v3("completed"))
_pg, _pe = arms("published")
_cg, _ce = arms("completed")
check("4.1", "completed: requests and httpx tasks with the published verdicts on both arms", 14,
      sum(1 for r in _rh if _cg[r["instance_id"]] == _pg[r["instance_id"]] and _ce[r["instance_id"]] == _pe[r["instance_id"]]))
check("4.1", "fastapi gold resolved: completed_noshadow / completed_pinned", "13 / 49",
      f"{S['verification_noshadow']['by_repo']['fastapi/fastapi']['gold_resolved']} / "
      f"{S['verification_pinned']['by_repo']['fastapi/fastapi']['gold_resolved']}")
check("4.1", "completed2_pinned: gold / empty / valid", "107 / 2 / 105", v3_runs("completed2_pinned"))
_g2, _e2 = arms("completed2_pinned")
check("4.1", "completed2_pinned: empty patch resolves", "fastapi_14077, fastapi_14851",
      ", ".join(sorted(i for i, v in _e2.items() if v)))
_fail = [r for r in RUNS if r["env"] == "completed2_pinned" and r["arm"] == "gold" and not r["resolved"]]
check("4.1", "completed2_pinned: failing fixes / of them naming a missing module or fixture", "22 / 0",
      f"{len(_fail)} / {sum(1 for r in _fail if r['missing_modules'] or r['missing_fixtures'])}")
_named = ["fastapi_14186", "fastapi_14356", "rich_3472", "rich_3486"]
_ids = {r["instance_id"] for r in _fail}
check("4.1", "completed2_pinned failing fixes: the four named tasks among them", ", ".join(_named),
      ", ".join(i for i in _named if i in _ids))
check("4.1", "completed2_pinned failing fixes besides those four: requests / rich / fastapi", "8 / 7 / 3",
      " / ".join(str(sum(1 for r in _fail if r["repo"] == repo and r["instance_id"] not in _named))
                 for repo in ("psf/requests", "Textualize/rich", "fastapi/fastapi")))
_ns = {r["instance_id"]: r["repo"] for r in RUNS if r["env"] == "completed_noshadow"}
_c2 = {r["instance_id"]: r["repo"] for r in RUNS if r["env"] == "completed2"}
check("3", "environments / completed_noshadow tasks / completed2 tasks (all fastapi)", "6 / 67 / 22",
      f"{len({r['env'] for r in RUNS})} / {len(_ns)} / {len(_c2)}",
      len({r["env"] for r in RUNS}) == 6 and len(_ns) == 67 and len(_c2) == 22
      and set(_ns.values()) | set(_c2.values()) == {"fastapi/fastapi"})
check("4.1", "official decisions / disagreements with the reimplementation", "1,210 / 0",
      f"{len(RUNS):,} / {sum(1 for r in RUNS if not r['agree'])}")

e = S["sample_eval_config"]["effective"]
check("4.2", "sample budget: minutes / tool calls / turns / command timeout", "1 / 10 / 50 / 60",
      f"{e['time_minutes']} / {e['tool_calls']} / {e['turns']} / {e['command_timeout_seconds']}")
check("4.2", "budget warning ever shown", False, S["sample_eval_config"]["budget_warning_ever_shown"])
check("4.2", "break-even decode rate, tokens/s", 68.3,
      S["sample_eval_config"]["break_even_decode_tokens_per_s_for_one_full_thinking_turn"])
mx = max(r["max_setup_command_s"] or 0 for r in RUNS)
check("4.2", "longest setup command, all runs (s)", "within 18", mx, mx <= 18)
r3480 = next(r for r in pub if r["instance_id"] == "rich_3480" and r["arm"] == "empty")
check("4.2", "rich_3480 target tests on the unfixed code (s)", 144, round(r3480["pytest_s"]))
hung = sorted(r["instance_id"] for r in pub if r["arm"] == "empty" and "pytest_verify" in (r["commands_timed_out"] or []))
check("4.2", "unfixed code hits the 300 s limit", "rich_3772, rich_4006", ", ".join(hung))

g_ = [k for k in sizes if k.startswith("graphs/")]
e_ = [k for k in sizes if k.startswith("embeddings/")]
check("4.3", "empty graph / embedding files, as first published", "129 of 256 / 129 of 256",
      f"{sum(1 for k in g_ if sizes[k] == 0)} of {len(g_)} / {sum(1 for k in e_ if sizes[k] == 0)} of {len(e_)}")
check("Abstract, 4.3", "tasks in class A / B / C or D, as first published", "34 / 26 / 69",
      f"{GA['all'].get('class_A', 0)} / {GA['all'].get('class_B', 0)} / "
      f"{GA['all'].get('class_C', 0) + GA['all'].get('class_D', 0)}")
_A = [r for r in ga_rows if cls[r["instance_id"]] == "A_intact"]
_B = [r for r in ga_rows if cls[r["instance_id"]] == "B_graph_only"]
_CD = [r for r in ga_rows if cls[r["instance_id"]] in ("C_embeddings_only", "D_neither")]
_tools = ("get_code_neighbors", "get_code_subgraph", "search_similar_code_symbol", "search_similar_code_phrase")

      "with status ok / classes C and D with no block, every graph call failing and charged", "34 / 26 / 69",
      f"{sum(1 for r in _A if r['as_shipped']['prompt_has_graph_block'] and all(r['as_shipped'][k]['status'] == 'ok' for k in _tools) and (r['as_shipped']['search_similar_code_symbol']['count'] or 0) > 0)} / "
      f"{sum(1 for r in _B if not r['as_shipped']['prompt_has_graph_block'] and r['as_shipped']['search_similar_code_symbol']['status'] == 'ok' and not r['as_shipped']['search_similar_code_symbol']['count'])} / "
      f"{sum(1 for r in _CD if not r['as_shipped']['prompt_has_graph_block'] and all(r['as_shipped'][k]['status'] != 'ok' for k in ('get_code_neighbors', 'get_code_subgraph', 'search_similar_code_symbol')) and r['as_shipped']['tool_calls_charged'] == 4)}")
check("4.3", "block and tools after repair (tasks)", 129, min(GA["all"]["repaired_block"], GA["all"]["repaired_neighbors_ok"]))
check("4.3", "tasks the sample prompt steers to search_similar_code", 122, S["sample_prompt_steers_to_search"])

      f"{GA['all']['shipped_phrase_search_results']} / {GA['all']['repaired_phrase_search_results']} / "
      f"{REISSUE['probe_phrase_search_results']}")

      f"{GA['all']['function_nodes']:,} / {GA['all']['async_def_nodes']:,} / {GA['all']['nodes_enclosing_async_def']:,}")
check("4.3", "changed sync functions in graph / changed async in graph", "379 of 388 / 0 of 56",
      f"{GA['all']['changed_sync_in_graph']} of {GA['all']['changed_sync']} / "
      f"{GA['all']['changed_async_in_graph']} of {GA['all']['changed_async']}")

      REISSUE["tasks_whose_commit_has_a_nonempty_graph_and_embedding"])

      len(json.loads((WORK / "g4_audit/audit/tasks_audit.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["columns"]))

grid = {(r["n"], r["flip_rate"]): r for r in P["grid"]}
F3 = (0.05, 0.10, 0.20)
check("4.5", "power / significance level of the model", "80% / 0.05 two-sided", P["model"],
      "power 0.80" in P["model"] and "alpha = 0.05 two-sided" in P["model"])
check("4.5", "MDE, 60 tasks per-task, f = 5/10/20%", "17.1 / 20.1 / 25.0",
      " / ".join(f"{grid[(60, f)]['mde_paired_points']}" for f in F3))

      " / ".join(f"{grid[(60, f)]['mde_aggregate_known_f_points']}" for f in F3))

      " / ".join(f"{grid[(129, f)]['mde_paired_points']}" for f in F3))
check("Abstract", "leaderboard's best case over f = 5 to 20%, 60 tasks (points, rounded)", "10 to 19",
      f"{round(grid[(60, 0.05)]['mde_aggregate_known_f_points'])} to {round(grid[(60, 0.20)]['mde_aggregate_known_f_points'])}")
pr = P["mde_under_prior_uniform_5_to_20pct"]
check("4.5", "MDE under the uniform 5-20% prior, 60 / 129 tasks", "21.4 / 12.7", f"{pr['60']} / {pr['129']}")
w = next(r for r in P["single_score_precision"] if r["n"] == 60 and r["resolved"] == 7)["wilson95"]
check("4.5", "Wilson 95% interval for 7 of 60 (%)", "5.8 to 22.2", f"{w[0]} to {w[1]}")
w = next(r for r in P["flip_rate_from_one_pair_of_runs"] if r["n"] == 129 and r["true_flip_rate"] == 0.10)["wilson95"]
check("4.5", "f from one pair of runs on 129 tasks at f = 10% (%)", "about 6 to 17", f"{w[0]} to {w[1]}",
      abs(w[0] - 6) <= 0.5 and abs(w[1] - 17) <= 0.5)
gap = max(abs(r["mde_paired_points"] - r["mde_paired_asymptotic_points"]) for r in P["grid"])
check("3", "largest gap, exact vs Connor's approximation (points)", "within 2.0", f"{gap:.1f}", gap <= 2.0 + 1e-9)

    ["| Section | Figure | Writeup | Regenerated | Agrees |", "|---|---|---|---|---|"]
    + [f"| {sec} | {what} | {quoted} | {value} | {'yes' if ok else '**NO**'} |" for sec, what, quoted, value, ok in CHECKS])))

    encoding="utf-8")

# CELL 36 markdown
## Outputs

Under `g4_audit/`: `audit/tasks_audit.csv`, `tasks_audit.json`, `summary.json`, `summary.md`,
`graph_audit.jsonl`, `graph_audit_summary.md`, `graph_audit_summary.json`, `gold_symbols.jsonl`,
`file_listing_used.json`; `power/power_table.csv`, `.json`, `.md`; `reissue/reissue_summary.json`;
and `writeup_figures.json`. All hold derived measurements only. The vendored code is under `g4src/`.

Not regenerated here: the verifier runs (`scripts/verify_scorer.py`, which builds a sandbox per
task and needs the harness wheels) and the harness tool probes (`scripts/audit_graphs.py`, which
imports the harness), for both releases. Both scripts are in the team's repository with their tests.
